# Revamp status log

This is the append-only coordination log for the six revamp threads (`engine`, `providers`, `ui-core`, `ui-screens`, `platform`, `integrator`). One entry per line, newest at the bottom, in the form `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM | <thread> | <TYPE> | <lane> | text`, with local Mac time, the thread name in lower case as listed, and `-` as the lane when an entry is about the whole thread. TYPE is one of NOTICE, FROZEN, HANDOFF-READY, LANE-DONE, REQUEST, BLOCKED-ON, CONTRACT-CHANGE-PROPOSED, CONTRACT-CHANGE-DONE, SLIP, READY-FOR-OWNER, APPROVED, MERGED, DECIDED. Never edit or delete an entry, yours or anyone's; correct one by appending a new entry that says so. Read it before each work package and each PR (`git fetch origin revamp`, then `git show origin/revamp:plans/STATUS.md`). Add entries as tiny commits pushed straight to `revamp`: `git pull --no-rebase origin revamp`, append your line at the end of the log, commit, `git push origin HEAD:revamp`; if the push is refused, pull again and retry, never force. No secrets, tokens, real addresses or real mail in any entry. Path ownership is in [OWNERS.md](OWNERS.md); `node plans/tools/queue.mjs` prints the owner's review queue from this file and flags malformed entries; the schedule and the rules behind this file are in [00-roadmap.md](00-roadmap.md) sections 1, 4, 7 and 12.

## Entry text, by type

Keep entries to one line. Address a REQUEST or BLOCKED-ON to a thread or lane by starting the text with `to <thread or lane>:`.

- `DECIDED`: a question whose answer was clear or had a default in the plans, the answer, and a one-line reason. Use it instead of waiting for the owner (owner's standing instruction, 2026-10-27). Questions that need a sign-in click, money, a design taste call the specs don't settle, a feature approval or anything on the hard-rules list still go to the owner; block only that item.
- `SLIP`: what moved, from which day to which day, and what it moves downstream.
- `READY-FOR-OWNER`: `F<n> <feature name> | <link to the ready post in the T3 thread> | lanes: <lane>, <lane>`.
- `APPROVED`: `F<n> | <lane>@<sha>, <lane>@<sha> | snapshot saved`.
- `MERGED` (feature PRs): `F<n> | <PR URL> | nothing material changed since <sha> | owner told`. The Integrator checks that an earlier READY-FOR-OWNER and APPROVED exist for the same feature and reverts the merge if not.
- `MERGED` (dependency and infrastructure PRs, posted by the integrator): `<PR URL> | <one-line summary>`.
- `CONTRACT-CHANGE-PROPOSED`: what is added and why, and the PR URL once it exists.

## How a PR gets into `revamp`

Every lane opens its PR from `rv/<lane>/<slug>` into `revamp`, after merging `revamp` into its branch. Nobody merges into `main`, ever.

1. **Dependency and infrastructure PRs** (anything another lane's "Needs" column names, and every PR from `eng-contract`, `eng-store`, `eng-views`, `prov-emu`, `ui-foundation`, `plat-ci` and `plat-release`) are merged by the integrator only. Before merging, the integrator confirms:
   - the PR touches only its lane's paths (the owners check, `scripts/audit/owners-check.mjs` once it lands; by hand against OWNERS.md until then);
   - `npm run typecheck`, `npm test` and `npm run check` pass at the PR head merged with current `revamp` (`npm run check` once `plat-ci` adds it);
   - the conformance suite passes on the stand-in engine, and the real-engine run fails only on its expected-fail list, which may shrink and never grow (once the suite exists);
   - the evidence (test output, numbers, screenshots where relevant) is attached to the PR, never committed;
   - an agent that did not write the code has reviewed it.
   - the PR says which models wrote its code (roadmap 5.1a). Code written by a free OpenCode model or a Cursor model merges only with passing tests and a review of the full diff by a Claude agent; without that review the integrator refuses the merge.
   Small PRs merge in arrival order, critical lanes first when several wait (`eng-store`, `prov-gmail`, `eng-views`, `ui-shell`, `ui-reader`). Squash merge, except Phase 0's mechanical restructure commits, which merge by merge commit and go into `.git-blame-ignore-revs`.
2. **Feature PRs** follow the same model rule, then the per-feature loop (roadmap 12.3): READY-FOR-OWNER, the owner's approval, APPROVED with SHAs, four deep review passes, the PR with `gh pr create --attach`, then the lead thread squash-merges it itself only if nothing material changed since the approval snapshot, and posts MERGED. After every feature merge the integrator runs the full suite, the conformance run and the owners check on `revamp`.
3. **Never self-merged:** any PR labelled `contract-change` (the integrator merges it), a PR that touches another lane's paths, and anything that would merge while `revamp` is red. Post a REQUEST to the integrator instead. A PR that touches `packages/contract/**`, `packages/engine/src/ports.ts`, `packages/engine/src/store/migrations/**` or `packages/engine/src/methods/index.ts` from any lane other than `eng-contract` (or `eng-store` for migrations) is refused. Contract changes are additive within `v:1`.
4. **Red `revamp`:** the integrator finds the PR that broke it and reverts that PR at once with a note to its lane. Nobody force-pushes a shared branch; fix forward or revert.

2026-10-28 14:26 | integrator | REQUEST | eng-store | to engine: #54 (74f8123) my Opus second review says FIX one thing before merge: ingest.ts:104-109 safeLane wraps all of makeInput(), which runs ~6 SQL reads; an SQLite error that auto-rolls-back the transaction (IOERR, NOMEM, INTERRUPT, FULL) is swallowed, the commit continues in autocommit, changes rows + meta.seq + commits(op_id) persist but the data writes do not, and a retry with the same opId returns replayed:true so the writes are lost. Run the SQL reads outside the try; catch only decodeBody (host codec/JSON) and laneFor(input). Add a regression test with a faked statement throwing an SQLite-style error: commit rolls back and the opId is not recorded. Follow-ups (not blocking, decide or note in DECISIONS): correspondent counts inflate when a swept/deleted message is stored again (commit-messages.ts:182 vs :290); a throwing lane ingest hook blocks that account's sync every retry (ingest.ts:41; eng-time should know); confirm draft revisions start at >=1 because the 0002 backfill gives old submissions revision 0. Everything else in the commit path (atomicity, rank_source, compaction floor, migration 0002 additivity, SQL binding, privacy, portability) held. Post READY with the new head; #57 re-checks after.

FIX one NEW hole introduced by the retry-on-existing-id change (account-connect.ts:36 + acceptNewAccount): a retry for address A reuses the starting row id X but sends no expectedSubject, so the host does not check identity; if the user signs in as B, no row matches B, the commit reuses opId connect:X which the store treats as already applied (no-op), then start(X) runs: row X still says A but the vault under X holds B's credential, so account A syncs B's mailbox (same if B matches another starting row Y: resume(Y) runs but the credential sits under X). Fix: when an earlier row is reused, send expectedSubject from its stored subject and run refuseOtherIdentity against storedIdentity(earlier.id) before resume or commit; refuse a match on a different row; add a test 'retry for A answered by B'. 2026-10-31 08:50 integrator REQUEST | to engine (eng-contract / eng-time, #153 at c5559256; Opus review VERDICT FIX; additive-only (every new member optional, packages/contract untouched, Gmail's 2-arg deleteDraft still typechecks), G3 expected-message-id sourcing and refusal handling, G4 exact-equality adoption in the same writeRow as `wrote`, body cap 1,000,000 and the sanitiser path, `supersedes` only moves the base and never removes, no new skips all PASS): ONE HIGH, a two-hop overwrite (draft-writes.ts L736, reached via L869): the 'upload running' proof (a) only shows the Message-ID is ours, not the content (the same weakness the PR fixed for finished uploads). After a retry of the same revision `uploading` stays set (retry, L383-384) and syncDraft reuses its internetId. Scenario: the draft is synced at A; our update lands as C (Message-ID Y) but the reply is lost, so `uploading` stays {Y, rev}; the fact for C, superseding A, correctly advances the base to C; the phone then edits C into D keeping Message-ID Y (JMAP/IMAP style, which DECISIONS expects); the fact for D supersedes C (now remote_message_id) and STILL matches uploading.internetId, so the base silently advances to D with no conflict; the retry uploads with baseRemoteMessageId=D, the provider accepts and the phone's edit is overwritten; a discard calls remove(R, D) (draft-sync.ts L484), the guard passes and D is deleted. Before this PR D was ignored as an echo, the retry's base stayed A and the provider answered conflict. Fix: allow at most ONE move per upload attempt: store the attempt's base in `uploading` and accept proof (a) only while row.remote_message_id is still that base and the fact supersedes it; any further hop needs holdsOurContent; add a test for the two-hop phone edit while the uncertain retry is pending. Low: (2) check w.text.length / w.html.length against the cap BEFORE paragraphs()/escaping (L612, L627; bodyOf runs up to 3 times per fact) and bound facts.ts L196-198; (3) add a one-line fake that IGNORES expectedMessageId (an old provider) to pin the fallback (the engine then makes the old 2-arg call and keeps changedElsewhere and the conflict-or-sent forget rule). Post READY with the new head; I re-run the check and an Opus re-check of the fix diff.

Fix: a same-revision retry keeps the FIRST attempt's base (`meta.uploading?.revision === row.revision ? meta.uploading.base : row.remote_message_id`), or null once the move is used; test the unanswered-retry phone edit AND the discard. (2, HIGH) one COALESCED fact covers both hops: history may report only the latest state, D superseding [A, C] or just [A] under Y (provider.ts L163-169: supersedes is a list), and the check at dw.ts L763 passes and the base jumps A -> D. Fix: accept proof (a) only when every superseded id is the base; store the upload's fingerprint (uploadOf already computes it) in `uploading` and require a delivered body to match it; a fact with no body gets no proof (the conflict-safe side). (3, MEDIUM, new) facts.ts L200-201 drops an over-limit html but keeps a text that fits, so the engine never sees the over-limit html, bodyOf falls back to the text (dw.ts L638) and the draft becomes complete: the next local edit uploads a body built from the text over the phone's rich html with no conflict. Fix: when ANY delivered source is over the limit carry no body at all (or leave the cap to candidate() alone). Simplest safe design, if proof (a) keeps growing: DROP proof (a) entirely and advance the base only through holdsOurContent (content fingerprint equals what we uploaded) or an owner decision; an unexplained fact is then a conflict, which is always the safe direction. Post READY with the new head; I re-run the check and a short Opus re-check of only the fix diff.
